===== M0009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.NamedType.resolveInternal(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/NamedType.java
Original range: 183-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve the referenced type within the enclosing scope.
   */
Method code:
183 |   @Override
184 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> enclosing) {
185 |     // TODO(user): Investigate whether it is really necessary to keep two
186 |     // different mechanisms for resolving named types, and if so, which order
187 |     // makes more sense. Now, resolution via registry is first in order to
188 |     // avoid triggering the warnings built into the resolution via properties.
189 |     boolean resolved = resolveViaRegistry(t, enclosing);
190 |     if (detectInheritanceCycle()) {
191 |       handleTypeCycle(t);
192 |     }
193 | 
194 |     if (resolved) {
195 |       super.resolveInternal(t, enclosing);
196 |       finishPropertyContinuations();
197 |       return registry.isLastGeneration() ?
198 |           getReferencedType() : this;
199 |     }
200 | 
201 |     resolveViaProperties(t, enclosing);
202 |     if (detectInheritanceCycle()) {
203 |       handleTypeCycle(t);
204 |     }
205 | 
206 |     super.resolveInternal(t, enclosing);
207 |     if (isResolved()) {
208 |       finishPropertyContinuations();
209 |     }
210 |     return registry.isLastGeneration() ?
211 |         getReferencedType() : this;
212 |   }

===== M0022 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 553-568
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
553 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
554 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
555 |       Set<String> directives = null;
556 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
557 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
558 |         if (directives == null) {
559 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
560 |         } else {
561 |           directives.add(directive);
562 |         }
563 |       }
564 | 
565 |       if (directives != null) {
566 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
567 |       }
568 |     }

===== M0024 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JoinOp.BinaryJoinOp.apply(List<L>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JoinOp.java
Original range: 36-50
Documentation:

Method code:
36 |     @Override
37 |     public final L apply(List<L> values) {
38 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!values.isEmpty());
39 |       int size = values.size();
40 |       if (size == 1) {
41 |         return values.get(0);
42 |       } else if (size == 2) {
43 |         return apply(values.get(0), values.get(1));
44 |       } else {
45 |         int mid = computeMidPoint(size);
46 |         return apply(
47 |             apply(values.subList(0, mid)),
48 |             apply(values.subList(mid, size)));
49 |       }
50 |     }

===== M0028 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // parameterized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isParameterizedType() || current.isParameterizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isParameterizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isParameterizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isParameterizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isParameterizedType());
224 |                 ParameterizedType parameterizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeParameterizedType();
225 |                 ParameterizedType parameterizedCurrent = current.toMaybeParameterizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (parameterizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(parameterizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   JSType alternateTypeParameter = parameterizedAlternate.getParameterType();
229 |                   JSType currentTypeParameter = parameterizedCurrent.getParameterType();
230 |                   if (currentTypeParameter.isEquivalentTo(parameterizedCurrent)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the parameterized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = parameterizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both parameterized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

===== M0041 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 477-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
478 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     int type = n.getType();
480 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
481 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
482 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     curNode = n;
486 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
487 | 
488 |     switch (type) {
489 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
490 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
495 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
496 |           // would no longer point to the true next
497 |           Node next = child.getNext();
498 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
499 |           child = next;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     curNode = n;
505 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
506 |   }

279 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
280 |     switch (c) {
281 |       case '@':
282 |       case '*':
283 |       case ',':
284 |       case '>':
285 |       case ':':
286 |       case '(':
287 |       case ')':
288 |       case '{':
289 |       case '}':
290 |       case '[':
291 |       case ']':
292 |       case '?':
293 |       case '!':
294 |       case '|':
295 |       case '=':
296 |       case EOF_CHAR:
297 |       case '\n':
298 |         return false;
299 | 
300 |       default:
301 |         return !isJSSpace(c);
302 |     }
303 |   }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 309-370
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
309 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
310 |     if (info != null) {
311 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
312 |       makesStructs = info.makesStructs();
313 |       makesDicts = info.makesDicts();
314 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
315 | 
316 |       if (makesStructs && !isConstructor) {
317 |         reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@struct", fnName);
318 |       } else if (makesDicts && !isConstructor) {
319 |         reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@dict", fnName);
320 |       }
321 | 
322 |       // base type
323 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
324 |         if (isConstructor) {
325 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
326 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
327 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
328 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
329 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
330 |           }
331 |         } else {
332 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
333 |         }
334 |       }
335 | 
336 |       // Implemented interfaces (for constructors only).
337 |       if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
338 |         if (isConstructor) {
339 |           implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
340 |           for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
341 |             JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
342 |             if (maybeInterType != null &&
343 |                 maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
344 |               implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
345 |             }
346 |           }
347 |         } else if (isInterface) {
348 |           reportWarning(
349 |               TypeCheck.CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, fnName);
350 |         } else {
351 |           reportWarning(CONSTRUCTOR_REQUIRED, "@implements", fnName);
352 |         }
353 |       }
354 | 
355 |       // extended interfaces (for interfaces only)
356 |       // We've already emitted a warning if this is not an interface.
357 |       if (isInterface) {
358 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
359 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
360 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
361 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
362 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
363 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
364 |           }
365 |         }
366 |       }
367 |     }
368 | 
369 |     return this;
370 |   }

===== M0057 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 111-154
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
111 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
112 |                                   String sourceString,
113 |                                   Config config,
114 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
115 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
116 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
117 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
118 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
119 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
120 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
121 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
122 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
123 | 
124 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
125 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
126 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
127 | 
128 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
129 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
130 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
131 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
132 | 
133 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
134 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
135 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
136 | 
137 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
138 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
139 |     try {
140 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
141 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
142 |       logger.info(
143 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
144 |     } finally {
145 |       Context.exit();
146 |     }
147 |     Node root = null;
148 |     if (astRoot != null) {
149 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
150 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
151 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
152 |     }
153 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
154 |   }

===== M0086 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 511-546
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
514 | 
515 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
516 | 
517 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
518 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
519 | 
520 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
521 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
522 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     curNode = n;
526 |     pushScope(n);
527 | 
528 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
529 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
530 |       // scope.
531 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
535 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
536 | 
537 |     // Args
538 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
539 | 
540 |     // Body
541 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
542 |             body.isBlock(), body);
543 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
544 | 
545 |     popScope();
546 |   }

120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {

129 |       case Token.EQ:

131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:

153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:

161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

1165 |           safeResolve(prototypeSlot.getType(), t, scope));
1166 |     }
1167 |

1169 |     // is handled further upstream.
1170 |     //
1171 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Handle this correctly if we have a UnionType.
1172 |     //
1173 |     // TODO(nicksantos): In ES3, the run-time coerces "null" to the global
1174 |     // activation object. In ES5, it leaves it as null. Just punt on this
1175 |     // issue for now by coercing out null. This is complicated by the
1176 |     // fact that when most people write @this {Foo}, they really don't
1177 |     // mean "nullable Foo". For certain tags (like @extends) we de-nullify
1178 |     // the name for them.
1179 |     JSType maybeTypeOfThis = safeResolve(typeOfThis, t, scope);
1180 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis != null) {
1181 |       maybeTypeOfThis = maybeTypeOfThis.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1182 |     }
1183 |     if (maybeTypeOfThis instanceof ObjectType) {
1184 |       typeOfThis = maybeTypeOfThis;
1185 |     }
1186 |

1188 |     ImmutableList.Builder<ObjectType> resolvedInterfaces =
1189 |         ImmutableList.builder();
1190 |     for (ObjectType iface : implementedInterfaces) {
1191 |       ObjectType resolvedIface = (ObjectType) iface.resolve(t, scope);
1192 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);
1193 |       changed |= (resolvedIface != iface);
1194 |     }
1195 |     if (changed) {
1196 |       implementedInterfaces = resolvedInterfaces.build();
1197 |     }
1198 | 
1199 |     if (subTypes != null) {
1200 |       for (int i = 0; i < subTypes.size(); i++) {
1201 |         subTypes.set(
1202 |             i, JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(subTypes.get(i).resolve(t, scope)));
1203 |       }
1204 |     }
1205 | 
1206 |     return super.resolveInternal(t, scope);
1207 |   }

===== M0097 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 288-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

1949 |       if (NodeUtil.isCallOrNewTarget(functionNode)) {
1950 |         iifeArgumentNode = functionNode.getNext();
1951 |       }
1952 | 
1953 |       Node body = astParameters.getNext();
1954 |       FunctionType functionType =
1955 |           JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(functionNode.getJSType());
1956 |       if (functionType != null) {
1957 |         Node jsDocParameters = functionType.getParametersNode();
1958 |         if (jsDocParameters != null) {
1959 |           Node jsDocParameter = jsDocParameters.getFirstChild();
1960 |           for (Node astParameter : astParameters.children()) {
1961 |             JSType paramType = jsDocParameter == null ?
1962 |                 unknownType : jsDocParameter.getJSType();
1963 |             boolean inferred = paramType == null || paramType == unknownType;
1964 | 
1965 |             if (iifeArgumentNode != null && inferred) {
1966 |               String argumentName = iifeArgumentNode.getQualifiedName();
1967 |               Var argumentVar =
1968 |                   argumentName == null || scope.getParent() == null
1969 |                   ? null : scope.getParent().getVar(argumentName);
1970 |               if (argumentVar != null && !argumentVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1971 |                 paramType = argumentVar.getType();
1972 |               }
1973 |             }
1974 | 
1975 |             if (paramType == null) {
1976 |               paramType = unknownType;
1977 |             }
1978 | 
1979 |             defineSlot(astParameter, functionNode, paramType, inferred);
1980 | 
1981 |             if (jsDocParameter != null) {
1982 |               jsDocParameter = jsDocParameter.getNext();
1983 |             }
1984 |             if (iifeArgumentNode != null) {
1985 |               iifeArgumentNode = iifeArgumentNode.getNext();
1986 |             }
1987 |           }
1988 |         }
1989 |       }
1990 |     } // end declareArguments

===== M0104 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

===== M0112 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0121 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1520-1674
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1520 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1521 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1522 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1523 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1524 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1525 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1526 | 
1527 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1528 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1529 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1530 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1531 | 
1532 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1533 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1534 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1535 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1536 |         }
1537 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1538 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1539 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1540 | 
1541 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1542 |         return createOptionalType(
1543 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1544 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1545 | 
1546 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1547 |         return createOptionalType(
1548 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1549 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1550 | 
1551 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1552 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1553 | 
1554 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1555 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1556 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1557 | 
1558 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1559 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1560 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1561 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1562 |           builder.addAlternate(
1563 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1564 |         }
1565 |         return builder.build();
1566 | 
1567 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1568 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1569 | 
1570 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1571 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1572 | 
1573 |       case Token.STRING:
1574 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1575 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1576 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1577 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1578 |         }
1579 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1580 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1581 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1582 |           if (typeList != null &&
1583 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1584 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1585 |             JSType parameterType =
1586 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1587 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1588 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1589 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1590 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1591 |               JSType indexType =
1592 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1593 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1594 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1595 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1596 |             }
1597 |           }
1598 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1599 |         } else {
1600 |           return namedType;
1601 |         }
1602 | 
1603 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1604 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1605 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1606 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1607 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1608 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1609 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1610 |           thisType =
1611 |               ObjectType.cast(
1612 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1613 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1614 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1615 |           if (thisType == null) {
1616 |             reporter.warning(
1617 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1618 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1619 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1620 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1621 |                 sourceName,
1622 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1623 |           }
1624 | 
1625 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1626 |           current = current.getNext();
1627 |         }
1628 | 
1629 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1630 | 
1631 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1632 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1633 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1634 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1635 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1636 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1637 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1638 |               } else {
1639 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1640 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1641 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1642 |               }
1643 |             } else {
1644 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1645 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1646 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1647 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1648 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1649 |                   reporter.warning(
1650 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1651 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1652 |                 }
1653 |               } else {
1654 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1655 |               }
1656 |             }
1657 |           }
1658 |           current = current.getNext();
1659 |         }
1660 | 
1661 |         JSType returnType =
1662 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1663 | 
1664 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1665 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1666 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1667 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1668 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1669 |             .build();
1670 |     }
1671 | 
1672 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1673 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1674 |   }

===== M0127 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionType.resolveInternal(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)

538 |   @Override
539 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
540 |     setResolvedTypeInternal(this); // for circularly defined types.
541 | 
542 |     boolean changed = false;
543 |     ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> resolvedTypes = ImmutableList.builder();
544 |     for (JSType alternate : alternates) {
545 |       JSType newAlternate = alternate.resolve(t, scope);
546 |       changed |= (alternate != newAlternate);

===== M0138 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 189-301
Documentation:

Method code:
189 |   @Override
190 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
191 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
192 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
193 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
194 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
195 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
196 |     // worthwhile.
197 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
198 |     Node condition = null;
199 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
200 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
201 | 
202 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
203 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
204 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
205 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
206 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
207 | 
208 |       switch (branch) {
209 |         case ON_TRUE:
210 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
211 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
212 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
213 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
214 | 
215 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
216 | 
217 |             if (item.isVar()) {
218 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
219 |             }
220 |             if (item.isName()) {
221 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
222 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
223 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
224 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
225 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
226 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
227 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
228 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
229 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
230 |                 }
231 |               }
232 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
233 |             }
234 |             newScope = informed;
235 |             break;
236 |           }
237 | 
238 |           // FALL THROUGH
239 | 
240 |         case ON_FALSE:
241 |           if (condition == null) {
242 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
243 |             if (condition == null && source.isCase()) {
244 |               condition = source;
245 | 
246 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
247 |               // of the loop.
248 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
249 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
250 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
251 |               }
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (condition != null) {
256 |             if (condition.isAnd() ||
257 |                 condition.isOr()) {
258 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
259 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
260 |               // scope on false.
261 |               //
262 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
263 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
264 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
265 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
266 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
267 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
268 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
269 | 
270 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
271 |               // of the loop.
272 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
273 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.isAnd() ?
274 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
275 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
276 |               }
277 |               newScope =
278 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
279 |                       condition,
280 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
281 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
282 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
283 |             } else {
284 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
285 |               // of the loop.
286 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
287 |                 conditionFlowScope =
288 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
289 |               }
290 |               newScope =
291 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
292 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
293 |             }
294 |           }
295 |           break;
296 |       }
297 | 
298 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
299 |     }
300 |     return result;
301 |   }

===== M0148 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1344-1373
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1344 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1345 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1346 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1347 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1348 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1349 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1350 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1351 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1352 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1353 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1354 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1355 |         parentNodeType == Token.PARAM_LIST ||
1356 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1357 |       return false;
1358 |     }
1359 | 
1360 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1361 |     if (type == null) {
1362 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1363 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1364 |       if (var != null) {
1365 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1366 |         if (varType != null) {
1367 |           type = varType;
1368 |         }
1369 |       }
1370 |     }
1371 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1372 |     return true;
1373 |   }

440 |     FunctionType REGEXP_FUNCTION_TYPE =
441 |       new FunctionType(this, "RegExp", null,
442 |           createArrowType(createOptionalParameters(ALL_TYPE, ALL_TYPE)),
443 |           null, null, true, true);
444 |     REGEXP_FUNCTION_TYPE.getInternalArrowType().returnType =
445 |         REGEXP_FUNCTION_TYPE.getInstanceType();
446 |

448 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.REGEXP_FUNCTION_TYPE, REGEXP_FUNCTION_TYPE);
449 | 
450 |     ObjectType REGEXP_TYPE = REGEXP_FUNCTION_TYPE.getInstanceType();
451 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.REGEXP_TYPE, REGEXP_TYPE);
452 | 
453 |     // String
454 |     FunctionType STRING_OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE =
455 |         new FunctionType(this, "String", null,
456 |             createArrowType(createOptionalParameters(ALL_TYPE), STRING_TYPE),
457 |             null, null, true, true);
458 |     ObjectType stringPrototype = STRING_OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE.getPrototype();
459 |     registerNativeType(
460 |         JSTypeNative.STRING_OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE, STRING_OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE);
461 |

463 |         STRING_OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE.getInstanceType();
464 |     registerNativeType(
465 |         JSTypeNative.STRING_OBJECT_TYPE, STRING_OBJECT_TYPE);
466 | 
467 |     // (null,void)
468 |     JSType NULL_VOID =
469 |         createUnionType(NULL_TYPE, VOID_TYPE);
470 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.NULL_VOID, NULL_VOID);
471 | 
472 |     // (Object,string,number)
473 |     JSType OBJECT_NUMBER_STRING =
474 |         createUnionType(OBJECT_TYPE, NUMBER_TYPE, STRING_TYPE);
475 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_NUMBER_STRING, OBJECT_NUMBER_STRING);
476 | 
477 |     // (Object,string,number,boolean)
478 |     JSType OBJECT_NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN =
479 |         createUnionType(OBJECT_TYPE, NUMBER_TYPE, STRING_TYPE, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
480 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN,
481 |         OBJECT_NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN);
482 | 
483 |     // (string,number,boolean)
484 |     JSType NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN =

486 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN,
487 |         NUMBER_STRING_BOOLEAN);
488 |

490 |     JSType NUMBER_STRING = createUnionType(NUMBER_TYPE, STRING_TYPE);
491 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.NUMBER_STRING, NUMBER_STRING);
492 | 
493 |     // Native object properties are filled in by externs...
494 | 
495 |     // (String, string)
496 |     JSType STRING_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE =
497 |         createUnionType(STRING_OBJECT_TYPE, STRING_TYPE);
498 |     registerNativeType(
499 |         JSTypeNative.STRING_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE, STRING_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE);
500 | 
501 |     // (Number, number)
502 |     JSType NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE =
503 |         createUnionType(NUMBER_OBJECT_TYPE, NUMBER_TYPE);
504 |     registerNativeType(
505 |         JSTypeNative.NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE, NUMBER_VALUE_OR_OBJECT_TYPE);
506 | 
507 |     // unknown function type, i.e. (?...) -> ?
508 |     FunctionType U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE =
509 |         createFunctionType(UNKNOWN_TYPE, true, UNKNOWN_TYPE);
510 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE, U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE);
511 | 
512 |     // unknown constructor type, i.e. (?...) -> ? with the Unknown type
513 |     // as instance type
514 |     FunctionType U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE =
515 |         // This is equivalent to
516 |         // createConstructorType(UNKNOWN_TYPE, true, UNKNOWN_TYPE), but,
517 |         // in addition, overrides getInstanceType() to return the NoObject type
518 |         // instead of a new anonymous object.
519 |         new FunctionType(this, "Function", null,
520 |             createArrowType(
521 |                 createParametersWithVarArgs(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
522 |                 UNKNOWN_TYPE),
523 |             UNKNOWN_TYPE, null, true, true) {
524 |           private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;
525 | 
526 |           @Override public FunctionType getConstructor() {
527 |             return registry.getNativeFunctionType(
528 |                 JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
529 |           }
530 |         };
531 | 
532 |     // The U2U_CONSTRUCTOR is weird, because it's the supertype of its
533 |     // own constructor.
534 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE, U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
535 |     registerNativeType(
536 |         JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE, U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
537 | 
538 |     FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE.setInstanceType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE);
539 |     U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE.setImplicitPrototype(FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE);
540 | 
541 |     // least function type, i.e. (All...) -> NoType
542 |     FunctionType LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE =
543 |         createNativeFunctionTypeWithVarArgs(NO_TYPE, ALL_TYPE);
544 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE, LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
545 | 
546 |     // the 'this' object in the global scope
547 |     FunctionType GLOBAL_THIS_CTOR =
548 |         new FunctionType(this, "global this", null,
549 |             createArrowType(createParameters(false, ALL_TYPE), NUMBER_TYPE),
550 |             null, null, true, true);
551 |     ObjectType GLOBAL_THIS = GLOBAL_THIS_CTOR.getInstanceType();
552 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS, GLOBAL_THIS);
553 | 
554 |     // greatest function type, i.e. (NoType...) -> All
555 |     FunctionType GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE =
556 |         createNativeFunctionTypeWithVarArgs(ALL_TYPE, NO_TYPE);
557 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE,
558 |         GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
559 |

561 |     // registerPropertyOnType about the bootstrapping process.
562 |     registerPropertyOnType("prototype", OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE);
563 |   }

Method code:
413 |   private void checkNoTypeCheckSection(Node n, boolean enterSection) {
414 |     switch (n.getType()) {
415 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
416 |       case Token.BLOCK:
417 |       case Token.VAR:
418 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
419 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
420 |         JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
421 |         if (info != null && info.isNoTypeCheck()) {
422 |           if (enterSection) {
423 |             noTypeCheckSection++;
424 |           } else {
425 |             noTypeCheckSection--;
426 |           }
427 |         }

     * Defines a function literal.

Method code:
770 |     void defineFunctionLiteral(Node n) {
771 |       assertDefinitionNode(n, Token.FUNCTION);
772 | 
773 |       // Determine the name and JSDocInfo and l-value for the function.
774 |       // Any of these may be null.
775 |       Node lValue = NodeUtil.getBestLValue(n);
776 |       JSDocInfo info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(n);
777 |       String functionName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(lValue);

785 |       // declaration. Otherwise, the declaration will happen in other
786 |       // code paths.

===== M0163 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 477-854
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @Override
478 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     JSType childType;
480 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
481 |     Node left, right;
482 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
483 |     boolean typeable = true;
484 | 
485 |     switch (n.getType()) {
486 |       case Token.CAST:
487 |         Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
488 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(expr));
489 | 
490 |         // If the cast, tightens the type apply it, so it is available post
491 |         // normalization.
492 |         JSType castType = getJSType(n);
493 |         JSType exprType = getJSType(expr);
494 |         if (castType.isSubtype(exprType)) {
495 |           expr.setJSType(castType);
496 |         }
497 |         break;
498 | 
499 |       case Token.NAME:
500 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
501 |         break;
502 | 
503 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
504 |         typeable = false;
505 |         break;
506 | 
507 |       case Token.COMMA:
508 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
509 |         break;
510 | 
511 |       case Token.TRUE:
512 |       case Token.FALSE:
513 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
514 |         break;
515 | 
516 |       case Token.THIS:
517 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
518 |         break;
519 | 
520 |       case Token.NULL:
521 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
522 |         break;
523 | 
524 |       case Token.NUMBER:
525 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
526 |         break;
527 | 
528 |       case Token.STRING:
529 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
530 |         break;
531 | 
532 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
533 |         typeable = false;
534 |         break;
535 | 
536 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
537 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
538 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
542 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
543 |         break;
544 | 
545 |       case Token.REGEXP:
546 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
547 |         break;
548 | 
549 |       case Token.GETPROP:
550 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
551 |         typeable = !(parent.isAssign() &&
552 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
553 |         break;
554 | 
555 |       case Token.GETELEM:
556 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
557 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
558 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
559 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
560 |         typeable = false;
561 |         break;
562 | 
563 |       case Token.VAR:
564 |         visitVar(t, n);
565 |         typeable = false;
566 |         break;
567 | 
568 |       case Token.NEW:
569 |         visitNew(t, n);
570 |         break;
571 | 
572 |       case Token.CALL:
573 |         visitCall(t, n);
574 |         typeable = !parent.isExprResult();
575 |         break;
576 | 
577 |       case Token.RETURN:
578 |         visitReturn(t, n);
579 |         typeable = false;
580 |         break;
581 | 
582 |       case Token.DEC:
583 |       case Token.INC:
584 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
585 |         checkPropCreation(t, left);
586 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
587 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.NOT:
591 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
592 |         break;
593 | 
594 |       case Token.VOID:
595 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
596 |         break;
597 | 
598 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
599 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
600 |         break;
601 | 
602 |       case Token.BITNOT:
603 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
604 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
605 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
606 |               childType.toString());
607 |         }
608 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
609 |         break;
610 | 
611 |       case Token.POS:
612 |       case Token.NEG:
613 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
614 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
615 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
616 |         break;
617 | 
618 |       case Token.EQ:
619 |       case Token.NE:
620 |       case Token.SHEQ:
621 |       case Token.SHNE: {
622 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
623 |         right = n.getLastChild();
624 | 
625 |         if (left.isTypeOf()) {
626 |           if (right.isString()) {
627 |             checkTypeofString(t, right, right.getString());
628 |           }
629 |         } else if (right.isTypeOf() && left.isString()) {
630 |           checkTypeofString(t, left, left.getString());
631 |         }
632 | 
633 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
634 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
635 | 
636 |         // We do not want to warn about explicit comparisons to VOID. People
637 |         // often do this if they think their type annotations screwed up.
638 |         //
639 |         // We do want to warn about cases where people compare things like
640 |         // (Array|null) == (Function|null)
641 |         // because it probably means they screwed up.
642 |         //
643 |         // This heuristic here is not perfect, but should catch cases we
644 |         // care about without too many false negatives.
645 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
646 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
647 | 
648 |         TernaryValue result = TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
649 |         if (n.getType() == Token.EQ || n.getType() == Token.NE) {
650 |           result = leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
651 |           if (n.isNE()) {
652 |             result = result.not();
653 |           }
654 |         } else {
655 |           // SHEQ or SHNE
656 |           if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
657 |                   rightTypeRestricted)) {
658 |             result = n.getType() == Token.SHEQ ?
659 |                 TernaryValue.FALSE : TernaryValue.TRUE;
660 |           }
661 |         }
662 | 
663 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
664 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
665 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
666 |         }
667 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
668 |         break;
669 |       }
670 | 
671 |       case Token.LT:
672 |       case Token.LE:
673 |       case Token.GT:
674 |       case Token.GE:
675 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
676 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
677 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
678 |           validator.expectNumber(
679 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
680 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
681 |           validator.expectNumber(
682 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
683 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
684 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
685 |           // OK.
686 |         } else {
687 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
688 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
689 |           // should match a string context.
690 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
691 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
692 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
693 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
694 |           message = "right side of comparison";
695 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
696 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
697 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
698 |         }
699 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
700 |         break;
701 | 
702 |       case Token.IN:
703 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
704 |         right = n.getLastChild();
705 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
706 |         validator.expectString(t, left, getJSType(left), "left side of 'in'");
707 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
708 |         if (rightType.isStruct()) {
709 |           report(t, right, IN_USED_WITH_STRUCT);
710 |         }
711 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
715 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
716 |         right = n.getLastChild();
717 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
718 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
719 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "deterministic instanceof yields false");
720 |         validator.expectActualObject(
721 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
722 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
726 |         visitAssign(t, n);
727 |         typeable = false;
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
731 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
732 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
733 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
734 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
735 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
736 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
737 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
738 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
739 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
740 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
741 |         checkPropCreation(t, n.getFirstChild());
742 |         // fall through
743 | 
744 |       case Token.LSH:
745 |       case Token.RSH:
746 |       case Token.URSH:
747 |       case Token.DIV:
748 |       case Token.MOD:
749 |       case Token.BITOR:
750 |       case Token.BITXOR:
751 |       case Token.BITAND:
752 |       case Token.SUB:
753 |       case Token.ADD:
754 |       case Token.MUL:
755 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
756 |         break;
757 | 
758 |       case Token.DELPROP:
759 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
760 |         break;
761 | 
762 |       case Token.CASE:
763 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
764 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
765 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
766 |         typeable = false;
767 |         break;
768 | 
769 |       case Token.WITH: {
770 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
771 |         childType = getJSType(child);
772 |         validator.expectObject(t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
773 |         typeable = false;
774 |         break;
775 |       }
776 | 
777 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
778 |         visitFunction(t, n);
779 |         break;
780 | 
781 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
782 |       case Token.LABEL:
783 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
784 |       case Token.SWITCH:
785 |       case Token.BREAK:
786 |       case Token.CATCH:
787 |       case Token.TRY:
788 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
789 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
790 |       case Token.BLOCK:
791 |       case Token.EMPTY:
792 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
793 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
794 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
795 |       case Token.THROW:
796 |         typeable = false;
797 |         break;
798 | 
799 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
800 |       case Token.DO:
801 |       case Token.IF:
802 |       case Token.WHILE:
803 |         typeable = false;
804 |         break;
805 | 
806 |       case Token.FOR:
807 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
808 |           Node obj = n.getChildAtIndex(1);
809 |           if (getJSType(obj).isStruct()) {
810 |             report(t, obj, IN_USED_WITH_STRUCT);
811 |           }
812 |         }
813 |         typeable = false;
814 |         break;
815 | 
816 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
817 |       case Token.AND:
818 |       case Token.HOOK:
819 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
820 |       case Token.OR:
821 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
822 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
823 |         } else {
824 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
825 |           if ((n.isObjectLit())
826 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
827 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
828 |           } else {
829 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
830 |           }
831 |         }
832 |         if (n.isObjectLit()) {
833 |           JSType typ = getJSType(n);
834 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
835 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n, typ);
836 |           }
837 |         }
838 |         break;
839 | 
840 |       default:
841 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
842 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
843 |         break;
844 |     }
845 | 
846 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
847 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
848 | 
849 |     if (typeable) {
850 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
851 |     }
852 | 
853 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
854 |   }

===== M0171 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo,AstNode,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 289-341
Documentation:

Method code:
289 |   private void validateTypeAnnotations(
290 |       JSDocInfo info, AstNode node, Node irNode) {
291 |     if (info.hasType()) {
292 |       boolean valid = false;
293 |       switch (node.getType()) {
294 |         // Casts are valid
295 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.LP:
296 |           valid = node instanceof ParenthesizedExpression;
297 |           break;
298 |         // Variable declarations are valid
299 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.VAR:
300 |           valid = true;
301 |           break;
302 |         // Function declarations are valid
303 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.FUNCTION:
304 |           FunctionNode fnNode = (FunctionNode)node;
305 |           valid = fnNode.getFunctionType() == FunctionNode.FUNCTION_STATEMENT;
306 |           break;
307 |         // Object literal properties and catch declarations are valid.
308 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NAME:
309 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty
310 |               || node.getParent() instanceof CatchClause
311 |               || node.getParent() instanceof FunctionNode;
312 |           break;
313 |         // Object literal properties are valid
314 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GET:
315 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.SET:
316 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NUMBER:
317 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.STRING:
318 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty;
319 |           break;
320 | 
321 |         // Property assignments are valid, if at the root of an expression.
322 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.ASSIGN:
323 |           if (node instanceof Assignment) {
324 |             valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent())
325 |                 && isPropAccess(((Assignment)node).getLeft());
326 |           }
327 |           break;
328 | 
329 |         // Property definitions are valid, if at the root of an expression.
330 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETPROP:
331 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETELEM:
332 |           valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent());
333 |           break;
334 |       }
335 |       if (!valid) {
336 |         errorReporter.warning(MISPLACED_TYPE_ANNOTATION,
337 |             sourceName,
338 |             node.getLineno(), "", 0);
339 |       }
340 |     }
341 |   }
