/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1106 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1107 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1108 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1109 | 
1110 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.isName() && scope.isGlobal();
1111 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1112 |           isGlobalVar &&
1113 |           (parent.isVar() ||
1114 |            parent.isFunction());
1115 | 
1116 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1117 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1118 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1119 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1120 |       if (n.isGetProp() && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1121 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1122 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1123 | 
1124 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1125 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1126 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1127 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1128 |         }
1129 |       }
1130 | 
1131 |       // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet. So read
1132 |       // the extern info from the node.
1133 |       boolean isExtern = n.isFromExterns();
1134 |       Var newVar = null;
1135 | 
1136 |       // declared in closest scope?
1137 |       CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1138 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1139 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1140 |         newVar = validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1141 |             sourceName, input, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1142 |       } else {
1143 |         if (type != null) {
1144 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1145 |         }
1146 | 
1147 |         newVar =
1148 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1149 | 
1150 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1151 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1152 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1153 |               (initialValue.isObjectLit() ||
1154 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1155 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1156 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1157 |           }
1158 |         }
1159 |       }
1160 | 
1161 |       // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1162 |       FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1163 |       if (fnType != null &&
1164 |           // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1165 |           !type.isEmptyType()) {
1166 | 
1167 |         // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance type
1168 |         // (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1169 |         // This doesn't apply to structural constructors (like
1170 |         // function(new:Array). Checking the constructed type against
1171 |         // the variable name is a sufficient check for this.
1172 |         if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1173 |             variableName.equals(fnType.getReferenceName())) {
1174 |           finishConstructorDefinition(n, variableName, fnType, scopeToDeclareIn,
1175 |                                       input, newVar);
1176 |         }
1177 |       }
1178 | 
1179 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1180 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1181 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1182 |         if (inferred) {
1183 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1184 |               type == null ?
1185 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1186 |               type,
1187 |               n);
1188 |         } else {
1189 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1190 |         }
1191 |       }
1192 | 
1193 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1194 |           && type != null
1195 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1196 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1197 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1198 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1199 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1200 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1201 |         globalThisCtor
1202 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn((type.toMaybeFunctionType()).getInstanceType());
1203 |       }
1204 |     }

443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());

===== M0185 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1937-1939
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1937 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1938 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1939 |   }

===== M0186 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1601-1670
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1601 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1602 |     FunctionType functionType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(n.getJSType());
1603 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1604 |     if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1605 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1606 |       if (baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1607 |           baseConstructor != null &&
1608 |           baseConstructor.isInterface()) {
1609 |         compiler.report(
1610 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1611 |                         "constructor", functionPrivateName));
1612 |       } else {
1613 |         if (baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE)) {
1614 |           ObjectType proto = functionType.getPrototype();
1615 |           if (functionType.makesStructs() && !proto.isStruct()) {
1616 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1617 |                                         "struct", functionPrivateName));
1618 |           } else if (functionType.makesDicts() && !proto.isDict()) {
1619 |             compiler.report(t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1620 |                                         "dict", functionPrivateName));
1621 |           }
1622 |         }
1623 |         // All interfaces are properly implemented by a class
1624 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1625 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1626 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1627 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1628 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1629 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1630 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1631 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1632 |               badImplementedType = true;
1633 |             }
1634 |           } else {
1635 |             badImplementedType = true;
1636 |           }
1637 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1638 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1639 |           }
1640 |         }
1641 |         // check properties
1642 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1643 |       }
1644 |     } else if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1645 |       // Interface must extend only interfaces
1646 |       for (ObjectType extInterface : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1647 |         if (extInterface.getConstructor() != null
1648 |             && !extInterface.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
1649 |           compiler.report(
1650 |               t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE,
1651 |                           "interface", functionPrivateName));
1652 |         }
1653 |       }
1654 | 
1655 |       // Check whether the extended interfaces have any conflicts
1656 |       if (functionType.getExtendedInterfacesCount() > 1) {
1657 |         // Only check when extending more than one interfaces
1658 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties
1659 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1660 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties
1661 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1662 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1663 |           currentProperties.clear();
1664 |           checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionPrivateName,
1665 |               properties, currentProperties, interfaceType);
1666 |           properties.putAll(currentProperties);
1667 |         }
1668 |       }
1669 |     }
1670 |   }

Method code:
525 |   private void handleFunction(Node node) {
526 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
527 |     Preconditions.checkState(node.getChildCount() >= 3);

===== M0209 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 654-742
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
654 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
655 |     if (this == that) {
656 |       return true;
657 |     }
658 | 
659 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
660 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
661 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
662 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
663 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
664 |         // with everyone.
665 |         return true;
666 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
667 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
668 |         // both unknown.
669 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
670 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
671 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
672 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
673 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
674 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
675 |         return false;
676 |       }
677 |     }
678 | 
679 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
680 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
681 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
682 |     }
683 | 
684 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
685 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
686 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
687 |     }
688 | 
689 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
690 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
691 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
692 |     }
693 | 
694 |     ParameterizedType thisParamType = toMaybeParameterizedType();
695 |     ParameterizedType thatParamType = that.toMaybeParameterizedType();
696 |     if (thisParamType != null || thatParamType != null) {
697 |       // Check if one type is parameterized, but the other is not.
698 |       boolean paramsMatch = false;
699 |       if (thisParamType != null && thatParamType != null) {
700 |         paramsMatch = thisParamType.getParameterType().checkEquivalenceHelper(
701 |             thatParamType.getParameterType(), eqMethod);
702 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY) {
703 |         paramsMatch = false;
704 |       } else {
705 |         // If one of the type parameters is unknown, but the other is not,
706 |         // then we consider these the same for the purposes of data flow
707 |         // and invariance.
708 |         paramsMatch = true;
709 |       }
710 | 
711 |       JSType thisRootType = thisParamType == null ?
712 |           this : thisParamType.getReferencedTypeInternal();
713 |       JSType thatRootType = thatParamType == null ?
714 |           that : thatParamType.getReferencedTypeInternal();
715 |       return paramsMatch &&
716 |           thisRootType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatRootType, eqMethod);
717 |     }
718 | 
719 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
720 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
721 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
722 |     }
723 | 
724 |     // Unbox other proxies.
725 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
726 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
727 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
728 |               that, eqMethod);
729 |     }
730 | 
731 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
732 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
733 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
734 |           eqMethod);
735 |     }
736 | 
737 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
738 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
739 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
740 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
741 |     return this == that;
742 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
544 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {

546 |          current != null;

548 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {

550 |       }
551 |     }
552 |     return false;
553 |   }

===== M0221 =====

203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1252 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1253 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1254 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1255 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {

1257 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1258 |       } else {
1259 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1260 |       }
1261 |     }
1262 |     return builder.build();
1263 |   }

395 |     JSType maybeThisType = null;
396 |     if (info != null && info.hasThisType()) {
397 |       // TODO(johnlenz): In ES5 strict mode a function can have a null or
398 |       // undefined "this" value, but all the existing "@this" annotations
399 |       // don't declare restricted types.
400 |       maybeThisType = info.getThisType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry)
401 |           .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
402 |     }
403 |     if (maybeThisType != null) {
404 |       thisType = maybeThisType;
405 |     }
406 | 
407 |     return this;
408 |   }

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the

225 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

628 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {

633 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
634 |                         lineno, charno);

637 | 
638 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {

642 |                       // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type
643 |                       // system.
644 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
645 |                         token = next();
646 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
647 |                           token = next();
648 |                         }

652 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
653 |                       } else if (type != null) {
654 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
655 |                         // already.
656 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
657 |                       }
658 |                     }

661 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
662 |                     // for handling properties of params.
663 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
664 |                       name = null;
665 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
666 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
667 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
668 |                             lineno, charno);
669 |                       } else {

671 |                             lineno, charno);
672 |                       }
673 |                     }

678 |                     continue retry;
679 |                   }

684 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
685 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =

688 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
689 |

692 |                           paramDescription);

743 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
744 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
745 |                     } else {
746 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
747 |                     }
748 | 
749 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
750 |                   } else {
751 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
752 |                   }
753 |                   continue retry;
754 | 
755 |                 case STABLEIDGENERATOR:
756 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordStableIdGenerator()) {
757 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.stableidgen",
758 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
759 |                   }
760 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
761 |                   continue retry;
762 | 
763 |                 case SUPPRESS:
764 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
765 |                   continue retry;
766 | 
767 |                 case TEMPLATE:
768 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
769 |                   List<String> names = Lists.newArrayList(
770 |                       Splitter.on(',')
771 |                           .trimResults()
772 |                           .split(templateInfo.string));
773 |

775 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
776 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
777 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeNames(names)) {
778 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
779 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
780 |                   }

782 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
783 |                   continue retry;
784 | 
785 |                 case IDGENERATOR:
786 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordIdGenerator()) {
787 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.idgen",
788 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
789 |                   }
790 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
791 |                   continue retry;
792 | 
793 |                 case VERSION:
794 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
795 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
796 | 
797 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
798 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
799 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
800 |                   } else {
801 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
802 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
803 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
804 |                     }
805 |                   }
806 | 
807 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
808 |                   continue retry;
809 | 
810 |                 case CONSTANT:
811 |                 case DEFINE:
812 |                 case RETURN:
813 |                 case PRIVATE:
814 |                 case PROTECTED:
815 |                 case PUBLIC:
816 |                 case THIS:
817 |                 case TYPE:
818 |                 case TYPEDEF:
819 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
820 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
821 | 
822 |                   Node typeNode = null;
823 |                   boolean hasType = lookAheadForTypeAnnotation();
824 |                   boolean isAlternateTypeAnnotation =
825 |                       (annotation == Annotation.PRIVATE ||
826 |                        annotation == Annotation.PROTECTED ||
827 |                        annotation == Annotation.PUBLIC ||
828 |                        annotation == Annotation.CONSTANT);
829 |                   boolean canSkipTypeAnnotation =
830 |                       (isAlternateTypeAnnotation ||
831 |                        annotation == Annotation.RETURN);
832 |                   type = null;
833 |                   if (hasType || !canSkipTypeAnnotation) {
834 |                     skipEOLs();
835 |                     token = next();
836 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
837 | 
838 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
839 |                       typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
840 |                     }
841 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
842 |                   }
843 | 
844 |                   // The error was reported during recursive descent
845 |                   // recovering parsing
846 |                   boolean hasError = type == null && !canSkipTypeAnnotation;
847 |                   if (!hasError) {
848 |                     // Record types for @type.
849 |                     // If the @private, @protected, or @public annotations
850 |                     // have a type attached, pretend that they actually wrote:
851 |                     // @type {type}\n@private
852 |                     // This will have some weird behavior in some cases
853 |                     // (for example, @private can now be used as a type-cast),
854 |                     // but should be mostly OK.
855 |                     if ((type != null && isAlternateTypeAnnotation)
856 |                         || annotation == Annotation.TYPE) {
857 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
858 |                         parser.addTypeWarning(
859 |                             "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
860 |                       }
861 |                     }
862 | 
863 |                     switch (annotation) {
864 |                       case CONSTANT:
865 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
866 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
867 |                               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
868 |                         }
869 |                         break;
870 | 
871 |                       case DEFINE:
872 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
873 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
874 |                               lineno, charno);
875 |                         }
876 |                         break;
877 | 
878 |                       case PRIVATE:
879 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
880 |                           parser.addParserWarning(
881 |                               "msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
882 |                               lineno, charno);
883 |                         }
884 |                         break;
885 | 
886 |                       case PROTECTED:
887 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
888 |                           parser.addParserWarning(
889 |                               "msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
890 |                               lineno, charno);
891 |                         }
892 |                         break;
893 | 
894 |                       case PUBLIC:
895 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
896 |                           parser.addParserWarning(
897 |                               "msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
898 |                               lineno, charno);
899 |                         }
900 |                         break;
901 | 
902 |                       case RETURN:
903 |                         if (type == null) {
904 |                           type = createJSTypeExpression(newNode(Token.QMARK));
905 |                         }
906 | 
907 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
908 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
909 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
910 |                           break;
911 |                         }
912 | 
913 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
914 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
915 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
916 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
917 | 
918 |                           String returnDescription =
919 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
920 |

922 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
923 |                                 returnDescription);
924 |                           }
925 | 
926 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
927 |                         } else {
928 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
929 |                         }
930 |                         continue retry;
931 | 
932 |                       case THIS:
933 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
934 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
935 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
936 |                         }
937 |                         break;
938 | 
939 |                       case TYPEDEF:
940 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
941 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
942 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
943 |                         }
944 |                         break;
945 |                     }
946 |                   }
947 | 
948 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
949 |                   continue retry;
950 |               }
951 |             }
952 |           }
953 |           break;
954 | 
955 |         case EOC:
956 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
957 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
958 |           }
959 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
960 |           return true;
961 | 
962 |         case EOF:
963 |           // discard any accumulated information
964 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
965 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
966 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
967 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
968 |           return false;
969 | 
970 |         case EOL:
971 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
972 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
973 |           }
974 |           token = next();
975 |           continue retry;
976 | 
977 |         default:
978 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
979 |             token = next();
980 |             continue retry;
981 |           } else {
982 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
983 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
984 |             continue retry;
985 |           }
986 |       }
987 | 
988 |       // next token
989 |       token = next();
990 |     }
991 |   }

===== M0249 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 692-789
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
692 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
693 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
694 |     /*
695 |      * This is the case where:
696 |      *
697 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
698 |      * the script.
699 |      *
700 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
701 |      * the caller of the function.
702 |      *
703 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
704 |      * back to the caller of the function.
705 |      *
706 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
707 |      * asked to traverse.
708 |      *
709 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
710 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
711 |      */
712 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
713 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
714 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
715 |       return null;
716 |     }
717 | 
718 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
719 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
720 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
721 |       case Token.IF:
722 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
723 |       case Token.CASE:
724 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
725 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
726 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
727 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
728 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
730 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
731 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
732 |           } else {
733 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
734 |           }
735 |         } else {
736 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
737 |         }
738 |         break;
739 |       case Token.FOR:
740 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
741 |           return parent;
742 |         } else {
743 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
744 |         }
745 |       case Token.WHILE:
746 |       case Token.DO:
747 |         return parent;
748 |       case Token.TRY:
749 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
750 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
751 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
752 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
753 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
754 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
755 |           }
756 |         // CATCH block.
757 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
758 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
759 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
760 |           } else {
761 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
762 |           }
763 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
764 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
765 |           if (cfa != null) {
766 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
767 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.ON_EX, finallyNode);
768 |             }
769 |           }
770 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |         }
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
775 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
776 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
777 | 
778 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
779 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
780 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
784 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
785 |     } else {
786 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transferred up the AST.
787 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
788 |     }
789 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.DataFlowAnalysis(ControlFlowGraph<N>,JoinOp<L>)

/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.
   *
   * @see #analyze()
   */
Method code:
120 |   DataFlowAnalysis(ControlFlowGraph<N> targetCfg, JoinOp<L> joinOp) {

122 |     this.joinOp = joinOp;
123 |     Comparator<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nodeComparator =
124 |       cfg.getOptionalNodeComparator(isForward());
125 |     if (nodeComparator != null) {
126 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newTreeSet(nodeComparator);
127 |     } else {
128 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
129 |     }
130 |   }

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0265 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 274-337
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
274 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
275 |     this.options = options;
276 |     if (errorManager == null) {
277 |       if (outStream == null) {
278 |         setErrorManager(
279 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
280 |       } else {
281 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =

284 |         setErrorManager(printer);

287 | 
288 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
289 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
290 |       options.checkTypes = true;

292 |       options.checkTypes = false;
293 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {

300 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
301 |     }
302 |

304 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
305 |       options.setWarningLevel(
306 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
307 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
308 |     }
309 | 
310 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
311 |       options.setWarningLevel(
312 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
313 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
314 |     }
315 | 
316 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
317 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
318 |     guards.add(
319 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
320 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
321 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
322 | 
323 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
324 | 
325 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
326 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
327 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
328 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
329 |     // error off.
330 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
331 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
332 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
333 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
337 |   }

===== M0305 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

===== M0309 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.NamedType.lookupViaProperties(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/NamedType.java
Original range: 266-300
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolves a type by looking up its first component in the scope, and
   * subsequent components as properties. The scope must have been fully
   * parsed and a symbol table constructed.
   * @return The type of the symbol, or null if the type could not be found.
   */
Method code:
266 |   private JSType lookupViaProperties( ErrorReporter t,
267 |       StaticScope<JSType> enclosing) {
268 |     String[] componentNames = reference.split("\\.", -1);
269 |     if (componentNames[0].length() == 0) {
270 |       return null;
271 |     }
272 |     StaticSlot<JSType> slot = enclosing.getSlot(componentNames[0]);
273 |     if (slot == null) {
274 |       return null;
275 |     }
276 |     // If the first component has a type of 'Unknown', then any type
277 |     // names using it should be regarded as silently 'Unknown' rather than be
278 |     // noisy about it.
279 |     JSType slotType = slot.getType();
280 |     if (slotType == null || slotType.isAllType() || slotType.isNoType()) {
281 |       return null;
282 |     }
283 |     JSType value = getTypedefType(t, slot, componentNames[0]);
284 |     if (value == null) {
285 |       return null;
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // resolving component by component
289 |     for (int i = 1; i < componentNames.length; i++) {
290 |       ObjectType parentClass = ObjectType.cast(value);
291 |       if (parentClass == null) {
292 |         return null;
293 |       }
294 |       if (componentNames[i].length() == 0) {
295 |         return null;
296 |       }
297 |       value = parentClass.getPropertyType(componentNames[i]);
298 |     }
299 |     return value;
300 |   }

===== M0310 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 128-179
Documentation:

Method code:
128 |     @Override
129 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
130 |       // null is the smallest value
131 |       if (p2 == null) {
132 |         if (p1 == null) {
133 |           return 0;
134 |         } else {
135 |           return P1_GT_P2;
136 |         }
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       // check level
140 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
141 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
142 |       }
143 | 
144 |       // sourceName comparison
145 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
146 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
147 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
148 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
149 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
150 |           return sourceCompare;
151 |         }
152 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
153 |         return P1_LT_P2;
154 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
155 |         return P1_GT_P2;
156 |       }
157 |       // lineno comparison
158 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
159 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
160 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
161 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
162 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
163 |         return P1_LT_P2;
164 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
165 |         return P1_GT_P2;
166 |       }
167 |       // charno comparison
168 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
169 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
170 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
171 |         return charno1 - charno2;
172 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
173 |         return P1_LT_P2;
174 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
175 |         return P1_GT_P2;
176 |       }
177 |       // description
178 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
179 |     }

===== M0317 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.finishConstructorDefinition(Node,String,FunctionType,Scope,CompilerInput,Var)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1206-1250
Documentation:

Method code:
1206 |     private void finishConstructorDefinition(
1207 |         Node n, String variableName, FunctionType fnType,
1208 |         Scope scopeToDeclareIn, CompilerInput input, Var newVar) {
1209 |       // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1210 |       FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1211 |       Property prototypeSlot = fnType.getSlot("prototype");
1212 | 
1213 |       // When we declare the function prototype implicitly, we
1214 |       // want to make sure that the function and its prototype
1215 |       // are declared at the same node. We also want to make sure
1216 |       // that the if a symbol has both a Var and a JSType, they have
1217 |       // the same node.
1218 |       //
1219 |       // This consistency is helpful to users of SymbolTable,
1220 |       // because everything gets declared at the same place.
1221 |       prototypeSlot.setNode(n);
1222 | 
1223 |       String prototypeName = variableName + ".prototype";
1224 | 
1225 |       // There are some rare cases where the prototype will already
1226 |       // be declared. See TypedScopeCreatorTest#testBogusPrototypeInit.
1227 |       // Fortunately, other warnings will complain if this happens.
1228 |       Var prototypeVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(prototypeName);
1229 |       if (prototypeVar != null && prototypeVar.scope == scopeToDeclareIn) {
1230 |         scopeToDeclareIn.undeclare(prototypeVar);
1231 |       }
1232 | 
1233 |       scopeToDeclareIn.declare(prototypeName,
1234 |           n, prototypeSlot.getType(), input,
1235 |           /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1236 |           superClassCtor == null ||
1237 |           superClassCtor.getInstanceType().isEquivalentTo(
1238 |               getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1239 | 
1240 |       // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1241 |       // it constructs itself.
1242 |       if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1243 |           !n.isFromExterns()) {
1244 |         compiler.report(
1245 |             JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1246 |                 fnType.isConstructor() ?
1247 |                 CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1248 |                 variableName));
1249 |       }
1250 |     }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 303-472

Method code:
303 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
304 |     switch (n.getType()) {
305 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
306 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
307 |         break;
308 | 
309 |       case Token.NAME:
310 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
311 |         break;
312 | 
313 |       case Token.GETPROP:
314 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
315 |         break;
316 | 
317 |       case Token.AND:
318 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
319 |             .createChildFlowScope();
320 |         break;
321 | 
322 |       case Token.OR:
323 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
324 |             .createChildFlowScope();
325 |         break;
326 | 
327 |       case Token.HOOK:
328 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
329 |         break;
330 | 
331 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
332 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
333 |         break;
334 | 
335 |       case Token.CALL:
336 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
337 |         break;
338 | 
339 |       case Token.NEW:
340 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
341 |         break;
342 | 
343 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
344 |       case Token.ADD:
345 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
346 |         break;
347 | 
348 |       case Token.POS:
349 |       case Token.NEG:
350 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
351 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
352 |         break;
353 | 
354 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
355 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
356 |         break;
357 | 
358 |       case Token.THIS:
359 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
360 |         break;
361 | 
362 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
363 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
364 |       case Token.LSH:
365 |       case Token.RSH:
366 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
367 |       case Token.URSH:
368 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
369 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
370 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
371 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
372 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
373 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
374 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
375 |       case Token.DIV:
376 |       case Token.MOD:
377 |       case Token.BITAND:
378 |       case Token.BITXOR:
379 |       case Token.BITOR:
380 |       case Token.MUL:
381 |       case Token.SUB:
382 |       case Token.DEC:
383 |       case Token.INC:
384 |       case Token.BITNOT:
385 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
386 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
387 |         break;
388 |

390 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
391 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
392 |         break;
393 | 
394 |       case Token.COMMA:
395 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
396 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
397 |         break;
398 | 
399 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
400 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
401 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
402 |         break;
403 | 
404 |       case Token.DELPROP:
405 |       case Token.LT:
406 |       case Token.LE:
407 |       case Token.GT:
408 |       case Token.GE:
409 |       case Token.NOT:
410 |       case Token.EQ:
411 |       case Token.NE:
412 |       case Token.SHEQ:
413 |       case Token.SHNE:
414 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
415 |       case Token.IN:
416 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
417 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
418 |         break;
419 | 
420 |       case Token.GETELEM:
421 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
422 |         break;
423 | 
424 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
425 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
426 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isGetProp()) {
427 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
428 |         }
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.SWITCH:
432 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
433 |         break;
434 | 
435 |       case Token.RETURN:
436 |         scope = traverseReturn(n, scope);
437 |         break;
438 | 
439 |       case Token.VAR:
440 |       case Token.THROW:
441 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.CATCH:
445 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
446 |         break;
447 | 
448 |       case Token.CAST:
449 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
450 |         break;
451 |     }
452 | 
453 |     // TODO(johnlenz): remove this after the CAST node change has shaken out.
454 |     if (!n.isFunction()) {
455 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
456 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
457 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
458 | 
459 |         // A stubbed type declaration on a qualified name should take
460 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
461 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
462 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
463 |             n.getParent().isExprResult()) {
464 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
465 |         }
466 | 
467 |         n.setJSType(castType);
468 |       }
469 |     }
470 | 
471 |     return scope;
472 |   }

===== M0327 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1803-1838
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   */
Method code:
1803 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1804 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1805 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1806 |     }
1807 | 
1808 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1809 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1810 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1811 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1812 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1813 |       skipEOLs();
1814 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1815 |         next();
1816 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1817 |       }
1818 |     }
1819 | 
1820 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1821 | 
1822 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1823 |       next();
1824 |       skipEOLs();
1825 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1826 |       if (memberType != null) {
1827 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1828 | 
1829 |         skipEOLs();
1830 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1831 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1832 |         }
1833 | 
1834 |         next();
1835 |       }
1836 |     }
1837 |     return typeNameNode;
1838 |   }

===== M0331 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3066-3087
Documentation:
/** Find the l-value that the given r-value is being assigned to. */
Method code:
3066 |   static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
3067 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
3068 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = isFunctionDeclaration(n);
3069 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {
3070 |       return n.getFirstChild();
3071 |     } else if (parent.isName()) {
3072 |       return parent;
3073 |     } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3074 |       return parent.getFirstChild();
3075 |     } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3076 |       return parent;
3077 |     } else if (
3078 |         (parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3079 |         parent.isOr() ||
3080 |         parent.isAnd() ||
3081 |         (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3082 |       return getBestLValue(parent);
3083 |     } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3084 |       return getBestLValue(parent);
3085 |     }
3086 |     return null;
3087 |   }

     * Resolve any stub declarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 427-495
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
427 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
428 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
429 |     if (argsParent == null) {
430 |       if (info == null) {
431 |         return this;
432 |       } else {
433 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
434 |       }
435 |     }
436 | 
437 |     // arguments
438 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
439 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
440 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
441 |     }
442 | 
443 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
444 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
445 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
446 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
447 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
448 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
449 |     boolean isVarArgs = false;
450 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
451 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
452 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
453 | 
454 |       // type from JSDocInfo
455 |       JSType parameterType = null;
456 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
457 |       isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
458 | 
459 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
460 |         parameterType =
461 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
462 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
463 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
464 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
465 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
466 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
467 |       } else {
468 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
469 |       }
470 | 
471 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
472 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
473 |           isOptionalParam,
474 |           isVarArgs);
475 | 
476 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
477 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
478 |       }
479 |     }
480 | 
481 |     // Copy over any old parameters that aren't in the param list.
482 |     if (!isVarArgs) {
483 |       while (oldParameterType != null && !isVarArgs) {
484 |         builder.newParameterFromNode(oldParameterType);
485 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
486 |       }
487 |     }
488 | 
489 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
490 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
494 |     return this;
495 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthrough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
797 |     switch (n.getType()) {
798 |       case Token.DO:
799 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
800 |       case Token.FOR:
801 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
802 |           return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
803 |         }
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
805 |       case Token.LABEL:
806 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
807 |       default:
808 |         return n;
809 |     }
810 |   }

===== M0385 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.buildAndRegister()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 582-644
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
582 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
583 |     if (returnType == null) {
584 |       // Infer return types.
585 |       // We need to be extremely conservative about this, because of two
586 |       // competing needs.
587 |       // 1) If we infer the return type of f too widely, then we won't be able
588 |       //    to assign f to other functions.
589 |       // 2) If we infer the return type of f too narrowly, then we won't be
590 |       //    able to override f in subclasses.
591 |       // So we only infer in cases where the user doesn't expect to write
592 |       // @return annotations--when it's very obvious that the function returns
593 |       // nothing.
594 |       if (!contents.mayHaveNonEmptyReturns() &&
595 |           !contents.mayHaveSingleThrow() &&
596 |           !contents.mayBeFromExterns()) {
597 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
598 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
599 |       }
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     if (returnType == null) {
603 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
607 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
608 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
609 |     }
610 | 
611 |     FunctionType fnType;
612 |     if (isConstructor) {
613 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
614 |     } else if (isInterface) {
615 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(
616 |           fnName, contents.getSourceNode());
617 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
618 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
619 |       }
620 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
621 |     } else {
622 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
623 |           .withName(fnName)
624 |           .withSourceNode(contents.getSourceNode())
625 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
626 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
627 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
628 |           .withTemplateKeys(templateTypeNames)
629 |           .build();
630 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
631 |     }
632 | 
633 |     if (implementedInterfaces != null) {
634 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     if (extendedInterfaces != null) {
638 |       fnType.setExtendedInterfaces(extendedInterfaces);
639 |     }
640 | 
641 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeNames();
642 | 
643 |     return fnType;
644 |   }

Original range: 233-258

/**
   * Resolves a named type by looking up its first component in the scope, and
   * subsequent components as properties. The scope must have been fully
   * parsed and a symbol table constructed.
   */
Method code:
233 |   private void resolveViaProperties(ErrorReporter t,
234 |                                     StaticScope<JSType> enclosing) {
235 |     JSType value = lookupViaProperties(t, enclosing);
236 |     // last component of the chain
237 |     if (value != null && value.isFunctionType() &&
238 |         (value.isConstructor() || value.isInterface())) {
239 |       FunctionType functionType = value.toMaybeFunctionType();
240 |       setReferencedAndResolvedType(
241 |           functionType.getInstanceType(), t, enclosing);

243 |       setReferencedAndResolvedType(

253 |       //
254 |       // So if the type does resolve to an actual value, but it's not named,
255 |       // then don't respect the forward declaration.
256 |       handleUnresolvedType(t, value == null || value.isUnknownType());
257 |     }

===== M0391 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1332-1334
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1332 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1333 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1334 |   }

===== M0399 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1176-1184
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1176 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1177 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1178 |       return -1;
1179 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1180 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1181 |     } else {
1182 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1183 |     }
1184 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)

Original range: 624-636
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
624 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
625 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
626 |     if (typeSet == null) {
627 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
628 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
632 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
633 | 
634 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
635 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
636 |   }

===== M0425 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

481 |     @Override
482 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
483 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
