===== M0439 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.inferArguments(Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 121-164
Documentation:
/**
   * Infers all of a function's arguments if their types aren't declared.
   */
Method code:
121 |   private void inferArguments(Scope functionScope) {
122 |     Node functionNode = functionScope.getRootNode();
123 |     Node astParameters = functionNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
124 |     Node iifeArgumentNode = null;
125 | 
126 |     if (NodeUtil.isCallOrNewTarget(functionNode)) {
127 |       iifeArgumentNode = functionNode.getNext();
128 |     }
129 | 
130 |     FunctionType functionType =
131 |         JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(functionNode.getJSType());
132 |     if (functionType != null) {
133 |       Node parameterTypes = functionType.getParametersNode();
134 |       if (parameterTypes != null) {
135 |         Node parameterTypeNode = parameterTypes.getFirstChild();
136 |         for (Node astParameter : astParameters.children()) {
137 |           Var var = functionScope.getVar(astParameter.getString());
138 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(var);
139 |           if (var.isTypeInferred() &&
140 |               var.getType() == unknownType) {
141 |             JSType newType = null;
142 | 
143 |             if (iifeArgumentNode != null) {
144 |               newType = iifeArgumentNode.getJSType();
145 |             } else if (parameterTypeNode != null) {
146 |               newType = parameterTypeNode.getJSType();
147 |             }
148 | 
149 |             if (newType != null) {
150 |               var.setType(newType);
151 |               astParameter.setJSType(newType);
152 |             }
153 |           }
154 | 
155 |           if (parameterTypeNode != null) {
156 |             parameterTypeNode = parameterTypeNode.getNext();
157 |           }
158 |           if (iifeArgumentNode != null) {
159 |             iifeArgumentNode = iifeArgumentNode.getNext();
160 |           }
161 |         }
162 |       }
163 |     }
164 |   }

===== M0440 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInferencePass.inferAllScopes(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInferencePass.java
Original range: 77-107
Documentation:
/** Entry point for type inference when running over part of the tree. */
Method code:
77 |   void inferAllScopes(Node node) {
78 |     // Type analysis happens in two major phases.
79 |     // 1) Finding all the symbols.
80 |     // 2) Propagating all the inferred types.
81 |     //
82 |     // The order of this analysis is non-obvious. In a complete inference
83 |     // system, we may need to backtrack arbitrarily far. But the compile-time
84 |     // costs would be unacceptable.
85 |     //
86 |     // We do one pass where we do typed scope creation for all scopes
87 |     // in pre-order.
88 |     //
89 |     // Then we do a second pass where we do all type inference
90 |     // (type propagation) in pre-order.
91 |     //
92 |     // We use a memoized scope creator so that we never create a scope
93 |     // more than once.
94 |     //
95 |     // This will allow us to handle cases like:
96 |     // var ns = {};
97 |     // (function() { /** JSDoc */ ns.method = function() {}; })();
98 |     // ns.method();
99 |     // In this code, we need to build the symbol table for the inner scope in
100 |     // order to propagate the type of ns.method in the outer scope.
101 |     (new NodeTraversal(
102 |         compiler, new FirstScopeBuildingCallback(), scopeCreator))
103 |         .traverseWithScope(node, topScope);
104 |     (new NodeTraversal(
105 |         compiler, new SecondScopeBuildingCallback(), scopeCreator))
106 |         .traverseWithScope(node, topScope);
107 |   }

===== M0442 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 440-465
Documentation:

Method code:
440 |   @Override
441 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
442 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
443 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
444 |     switch (n.getType()) {
445 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
446 |         // normal type checking
447 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
448 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
449 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
450 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
451 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
452 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
453 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
454 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
455 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
456 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
457 |         }
458 | 
459 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
460 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
461 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
462 |         break;
463 |     }
464 |     return true;
465 |   }

===== M0446 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1489-1538
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1489 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1490 |     if (type != node.getType()
1491 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1492 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1493 |       return false;
1494 |     }
1495 | 
1496 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1497 |       return false;
1498 |     }
1499 | 
1500 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1501 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1502 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1503 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1504 |         return false;
1505 |       }
1506 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1507 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1508 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1509 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1510 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1511 |           return false;
1512 |         }
1513 |       }
1514 | 
1515 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1516 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1517 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1518 |         return false;
1519 |       }
1520 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1521 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1522 |         return false;
1523 |       }
1524 |     }
1525 | 
1526 |     if (recurse) {
1527 |       Node n, n2;
1528 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1529 |            n != null;
1530 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1531 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1532 |           return false;
1533 |         }
1534 |       }
1535 |     }
1536 | 
1537 |     return true;
1538 |   }

===== M0475 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 233-291
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
233 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
234 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
235 |     if (oldType == null) {
236 |       return this;
237 |     }
238 | 
239 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
240 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
241 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
242 |       // Not a function literal.
243 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
244 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
245 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
246 |       }
247 |     } else {
248 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
249 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
250 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
251 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
252 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
253 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
254 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
255 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
256 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
257 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
258 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
259 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
260 | 
261 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
262 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
263 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
264 | 
265 |           // The subclass method might write its var_args as individual
266 |           // arguments.
267 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
268 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
269 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
270 |           }
271 |         } else {
272 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
273 |               paramBuilder,
274 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
275 |               warnedAboutArgList,
276 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
277 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
278 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
279 |         }
280 |       }
281 | 
282 |       // Clone any remaining params that aren't in the function literal,
283 |       // but make them optional.
284 |       while (oldParams.hasNext()) {
285 |         paramBuilder.newOptionalParameterFromNode(oldParams.next());
286 |       }
287 | 
288 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
289 |     }
290 |     return this;
291 |   }

571 |     private void attachLiteralTypes(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
572 |       switch (n.getType()) {

575 |           break;

578 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
579 |           break;
580 |

582 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
583 |           break;
584 |

586 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
587 |           break;

590 |         case Token.FALSE:
591 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));

595 |           n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));

602 |             if (lentObjectLiterals == null) {

604 |             }
605 |             lentObjectLiterals.add(n);
606 |           } else {
607 |             defineObjectLiteral(n);
608 |           }
609 |           break;
610 |

612 |           // we will need to put ARRAYLIT here as well.
613 |       }
614 |     }

===== M0492 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

===== M0524 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 317-364
Documentation:

Method code:
317 |   @Override
318 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
319 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
320 |       return true;
321 |     }
322 | 
323 |     // Union types
324 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
325 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
326 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
327 |       return false;
328 |     }
329 | 
330 |     // record types
331 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
332 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     // Interfaces
336 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
337 |     // to the interface instance.
338 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
339 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
340 | 
341 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
342 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
343 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
344 |           return true;
345 |         }
346 |       }
347 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
348 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
349 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
350 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
351 |           return true;
352 |         }
353 |       }
354 |     }
355 | 
356 |     // other prototype based objects
357 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
358 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
359 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
360 |       // to avoid guessing.
361 |       return true;
362 |     }
363 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
364 |   }

===== M0549 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1340-1376
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1340 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1341 |     // unknown
1342 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1343 |       return true;
1344 |     }
1345 |     // all type
1346 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1347 |       return true;
1348 |     }
1349 |     // equality
1350 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1351 |       return true;
1352 |     }
1353 |     // unions
1354 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1355 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1356 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1357 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1358 |           return true;
1359 |         }
1360 |       }
1361 |       return false;
1362 |     }
1363 | 
1364 |     // parameterized types.
1365 |     if (thisType.isParameterizedType()) {
1366 |       return thisType.toMaybeParameterizedType().isParameterizeSubtypeOf(
1367 |           thatType);
1368 |     }
1369 | 
1370 |     // proxy types
1371 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1372 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1373 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1374 |     }
1375 |     return false;
1376 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeNameAnnotation(JsDocToken)

Original range: 1648-1665
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeNameAnnotation := TypeName | '{' TypeName '}'
   */
Method code:
1648 |   private Node parseTypeNameAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1649 |     if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1650 |       skipEOLs();

1652 |       if (typeNode != null) {
1653 |         skipEOLs();

1655 |           reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1656 |         } else {
1657 |           next();
1658 |         }
1659 |       }
1660 |

1662 |     } else {
1663 |       return parseTypeName(token);
1664 |     }
1665 |   }

===== M0552 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3035-3063
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3035 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3036 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3037 |     if (info == null) {
3038 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3039 |       if (parent == null) {
3040 |         return null;
3041 |       }
3042 | 
3043 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3044 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3045 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3046 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3047 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3048 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3049 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3050 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3051 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3052 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3053 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3054 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3055 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3056 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3057 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3058 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3059 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3060 |       }
3061 |     }
3062 |     return info;
3063 |   }

Method code:
179 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
180 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
181 |                                    String sourceString,
182 |                                    Config config,
183 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {

187 | 
188 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {

190 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.JSDOC &&
191 |             !irFactory.parsedComments.contains(comment)) {
192 |           irFactory.handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(comment, irNode);
193 |         } else if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.BLOCK_COMMENT) {
194 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);

196 |       }
197 |     }
198 | 
199 |     irFactory.setFileOverviewJsDoc(irNode);
200 | 
201 |     return irNode;
202 |   }

===== M0558 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0561 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 564-584
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
564 |   @Override
565 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
566 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
567 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
568 |     if (unknown) {
569 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
570 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
571 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
572 |         unknown = false;
573 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
574 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
575 |             unknown = true;
576 |             break;
577 |           }
578 |         }
579 |       } else {
580 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
581 |       }
582 |     }
583 |     return unknown;
584 |   }

   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.
   */

363 |   public ObjectType getPrototype() {

365 |     if (prototypeSlot == null) {

381 |             null);

===== M0598 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.resolve(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1419-1432
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.
   */

1422 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);

===== M0602 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 512-569
Documentation:

Method code:
512 |     @Override
513 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
514 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
515 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
516 | 
517 |       switch (n.getType()) {
518 |         case Token.CALL:
519 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
520 |           checkForCallingConventionDefiningCalls(n, delegateCallingConventions);
521 |           break;
522 | 
523 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
524 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
525 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
526 |           }
527 | 
528 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
529 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
530 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n);
531 |           }
532 |           break;
533 | 
534 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
535 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
536 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
537 |           if (firstChild.isGetProp() &&
538 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
539 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
540 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
541 |           }
542 |           break;
543 | 
544 |         case Token.CATCH:
545 |           defineCatch(n);
546 |           break;
547 | 
548 |         case Token.VAR:
549 |           defineVar(n);
550 |           break;
551 | 
552 |         case Token.GETPROP:
553 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
554 |           if (parent.isExprResult() &&
555 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
556 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
557 |           }
558 |           break;
559 |       }
560 | 
561 |       // Analyze any @lends object literals in this statement.
562 |       if (n.getParent() != null && NodeUtil.isStatement(n) &&
563 |           lentObjectLiterals != null) {
564 |         for (Node objLit : lentObjectLiterals) {
565 |           defineObjectLiteral(objLit);
566 |         }
567 |         lentObjectLiterals.clear();
568 |       }
569 |     }

===== M0618 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 709-791
Documentation:

Method code:
709 |     @Override
710 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
711 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
712 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
713 |       if (name == null) {
714 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
715 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
716 |           errorReporter.error(
717 |             "unnamed function statement",
718 |             sourceName,
719 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
720 | 
721 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
722 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
723 |         }
724 |         name = new Name();
725 |         name.setIdentifier("");
726 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
727 |       }
728 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
729 |       Node newName = transform(name);
730 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
731 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
732 |         // declaration.
733 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
734 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
735 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
736 |         // same line as the function keyword.
737 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
738 |             functionNode.getLp();
739 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
740 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
741 |       }
742 | 
743 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
744 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
745 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
746 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
747 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
748 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
749 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
750 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
751 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
752 |       if (fnName != null) {
753 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
754 |       } else {
755 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
756 |       }
757 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
758 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
759 | 
760 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
761 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
762 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
763 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
764 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
765 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
766 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
767 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
768 |         } else {
769 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
770 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
771 |           // (an error has already been reported).
772 |           Preconditions.checkState(
773 |               config.isIdeMode
774 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
775 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
776 |         }
777 |       }
778 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
779 | 
780 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
781 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
782 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
783 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
784 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
785 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
786 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
787 |       }
788 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
789 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
790 |      return node;
791 |     }

785 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
786 |     if (item == null) {

===== M0636 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 396-421
Documentation:

Method code:
396 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
397 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
398 |     // two cases:
399 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
400 |     // and
401 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
402 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
403 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
404 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
405 |     //
406 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
407 |     //
408 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
409 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
410 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
411 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
412 |     //
413 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
414 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
415 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
416 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
417 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
418 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
419 |     }
420 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
421 |   }

===== M0639 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 197-255
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
197 |   @Override
198 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
199 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
200 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
201 |     // show up in the type registry.
202 |     Scope newScope = null;
203 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
204 |     if (parent == null) {
205 |       JSType globalThis =
206 |           typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
207 | 
208 |       // Mark the main root, the externs root, and the src root
209 |       // with the global this type.
210 |       root.setJSType(globalThis);
211 |       root.getFirstChild().setJSType(globalThis);
212 |       root.getLastChild().setJSType(globalThis);
213 | 
214 |       // Run a first-order analysis over the syntax tree.
215 |       (new FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer(compiler, functionAnalysisResults))
216 |           .process(root.getFirstChild(), root.getLastChild());
217 | 
218 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
219 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
220 | 
221 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
222 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
223 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
224 |     } else {
225 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
226 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
227 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
228 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
229 |     }
230 | 
231 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
232 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
233 | 
234 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
235 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
236 |     // build properties on.
237 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
238 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
239 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
240 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
241 |         JSType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
242 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
243 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
244 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
245 |         }
246 |       }
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if (parent == null) {
250 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
251 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
252 |           delegateCallingConventions);
253 |     }
254 |     return newScope;
255 |   }

===== M0647 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1548-1563
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1548 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1549 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1550 |       String name = getString();
1551 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1552 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1553 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1554 |       if (left == null) {
1555 |         return null;
1556 |       }
1557 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1558 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1559 |       return "this";
1560 |     } else {
1561 |       return null;
1562 |     }
1563 |   }

===== M0656 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.detectImplicitPrototypeCycle()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 176-196
Documentation:
/**
   * Detects a cycle in the implicit prototype chain. This method accesses
   * the {@link #getImplicitPrototype()} method and must therefore be
   * invoked only after the object is sufficiently initialized to respond to
   * calls to this method.<p>
   *
   * @return True iff an implicit prototype cycle was detected.
   */
Method code:
176 |   final boolean detectImplicitPrototypeCycle() {
177 |     // detecting cycle
178 |     this.visited = true;
179 |     ObjectType p = getImplicitPrototype();
180 |     while (p != null) {
181 |       if (p.visited) {
182 |         return true;
183 |       } else {
184 |         p.visited = true;
185 |       }
186 |       p = p.getImplicitPrototype();
187 |     }
188 | 
189 |     // clean up
190 |     p = this;
191 |     do {
192 |       p.visited = false;
193 |       p = p.getImplicitPrototype();
194 |     } while (p != null);
195 |     return false;
196 |   }

124 |   private JSError makeError(String message, String sourceName, int line,
125 |       int lineOffset, CheckLevel defaultLevel) {
126 | 
127 |     // Try to see if the message is one of the rhino errors we want to
128 |     // expose as DiagnosticType by matching it with the regex key.

===== M0710 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 309-316
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
310 |     if (c <= 127) {
311 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
312 |     } else {
313 |       return c == 0xA0
314 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
315 |     }
316 |   }

===== M0711 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1569-1580
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns whether a node corresponds to a simple or a qualified name, such as
   * <code>x</code> or <code>a.b.c</code> or <code>this.a</code>.
   */
Method code:
1569 |   public boolean isQualifiedName() {
1570 |     switch (getType()) {
1571 |       case Token.NAME:

1577 |       default:

464 |     void resolveTypes() {
465 |       // Resolve types and attach them to nodes.
466 |       for (DeferredSetType deferred : deferredSetTypes) {
467 |         deferred.resolve(scope);
468 |       }
469 |

474 |       }

477 |       // are unknown.

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 941-965
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve all the unresolved types in the given scope.
   */
Method code:
941 |   public void resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
942 |     for (NamedType type : unresolvedNamedTypes.get(scope)) {
943 |       type.resolve(reporter, scope);
944 |     }
945 | 
946 |     resolvedNamedTypes.putAll(scope, unresolvedNamedTypes.removeAll(scope));
947 | 
948 |     if (scope != null && scope.getParentScope() == null) {
949 |       // By default, the global "this" type is just an anonymous object.
950 |       // If the user has defined a Window type, make the Window the
951 |       // implicit prototype of "this".
952 |       PrototypeObjectType globalThis = (PrototypeObjectType) getNativeType(
953 |           JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
954 |       JSType windowType = getType("Window");
955 |       if (globalThis.isUnknownType()) {
956 |         ObjectType windowObjType = ObjectType.cast(windowType);
957 |         if (windowObjType != null) {
958 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(windowObjType);
959 |         } else {
960 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(
961 |               getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE));
962 |         }
963 |       }
964 |     }
965 |   }

===== M0730 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 853-961
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the JsDoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
853 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
854 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
855 |         @Nullable String name,
856 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
857 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
858 | 
859 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
860 | 
861 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
862 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
863 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
864 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
865 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
866 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
867 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
868 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
869 |             functionType = aliasedType;
870 | 
871 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
872 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
873 |             }
874 |           }
875 |         }
876 |       }
877 | 
878 |       if (functionType == null) {
879 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
880 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
881 |             rValue != null && rValue.isFunction();
882 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
883 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
884 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
885 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
886 | 
887 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
888 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
889 | 
890 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
891 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
892 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
893 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
894 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
895 |           }
896 |         }
897 | 
898 |         if (functionType == null) {
899 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
900 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
901 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
902 |           Var ownerVar = null;
903 |           String propName = null;
904 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
905 |           if (ownerName != null) {
906 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
907 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
908 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
909 |             }
910 |             if (name != null) {
911 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
912 |             }
913 |           }
914 | 
915 |           FunctionType overriddenType = null;
916 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
917 |             overriddenType = findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
918 |           }
919 | 
920 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
921 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
922 |                   scope)
923 |               .setContents(getFunctionAnalysisResults(fnRoot))
924 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenType, parametersNode)
925 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
926 |               .inferReturnType(info)
927 |               .inferInheritance(info);
928 | 
929 |           // Infer the context type.
930 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
931 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType() &&
932 |               ownerType.getOwnerFunction().hasInstanceType()) {
933 |             builder.inferThisType(
934 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
935 |             searchedForThisType = true;
936 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.isThis()) {
937 |             // If 'this' has a type, use that instead.
938 |             // This is a hack, necessary because CollectProperties (below)
939 |             // doesn't run with the scope that it's building,
940 |             // so scope.getTypeOfThis() will be wrong.
941 |             JSType injectedThisType = ownerNode.getJSType();
942 |             builder.inferThisType(
943 |                 info,
944 |                 injectedThisType == null ?
945 |                 scope.getTypeOfThis() : injectedThisType);
946 |             searchedForThisType = true;
947 |           }
948 | 
949 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
950 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
951 |           }
952 | 
953 |           functionType = builder
954 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
955 |               .buildAndRegister();
956 |         }
957 |       }
958 | 
959 |       // all done
960 |       return functionType;
961 |     }

Documentation:

Method code:
638 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
639 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
640 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
641 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
642 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
643 |       if (typeSet == null) {
644 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
645 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
646 |       }
647 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
648 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
649 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
650 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
651 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
652 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
653 |       for (JSType alternate : type.toMaybeUnionType().getAlternates()) {
654 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
655 |       }
656 |     }
657 |   }

288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;

303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);

===== M0765 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 258-308
Documentation:

Method code:
258 |   @Override
259 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
260 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
261 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
262 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
263 |         return true;
264 |       }
265 | 
266 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
267 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
268 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate through all
269 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
270 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
271 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
272 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
273 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
274 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
275 |         return false;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
279 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
280 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
281 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
282 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
283 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
284 |             return false;
285 |           }
286 |         }
287 | 
288 |         return true;
289 |       }
290 | 
291 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
292 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
293 | 
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
299 |       }
300 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
301 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
302 |           return false;
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |       return true;
306 |     }
307 |     return false;
308 |   }

/**
   * Check whether there's any property conflict for for a particular super
   * interface
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context
   * @param n The node being visited
   * @param functionName The function name being checked
   * @param properties The property names in the super interfaces that have
   * been visited
   * @param currentProperties The property names in the super interface
   * that have been visited
   * @param interfaceType The super interface that is being visited
   */
Method code:
1566 |   private void checkInterfaceConflictProperties(NodeTraversal t, Node n,
1567 |       String functionName, HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties,
1568 |       HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties,
1569 |       ObjectType interfaceType) {
1570 |     ObjectType implicitProto = interfaceType.getImplicitPrototype();
1571 |     Set<String> currentPropertyNames;
1572 |       // This can be the case if interfaceType is proxy to a non-existent
1573 |       // object (which is a bad type annotation, but shouldn't crash).
1574 |       currentPropertyNames = implicitProto.getOwnPropertyNames();
1575 |     for (String name : currentPropertyNames) {
1576 |       ObjectType oType = properties.get(name);
1577 |       if (oType != null) {
1578 |         if (!interfaceType.getPropertyType(name).isEquivalentTo(
1579 |             oType.getPropertyType(name))) {
1580 |           compiler.report(
1581 |               t.makeError(n, INCOMPATIBLE_EXTENDED_PROPERTY_TYPE,
1582 |                   functionName, name, oType.toString(),
1583 |                   interfaceType.toString()));
1584 |         }
1585 |       }
1586 |       currentProperties.put(name, interfaceType);
1587 |     }
1588 |     for (ObjectType iType : interfaceType.getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
1589 |       checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionName, properties,
1590 |           currentProperties, iType);
1591 |     }
1592 |   }

===== M0769 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 2024-2075
Documentation:

Method code:
2024 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
2025 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
2026 |         return;
2027 |       }
2028 | 
2029 |       if (n.isReturn() && n.getFirstChild() != null) {
2030 |         data.get(t.getScopeRoot()).recordNonEmptyReturn();
2031 |       }
2032 | 
2033 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() <= 1) {
2034 |         // The first-order function analyzer looks at two types of variables:
2035 |         //
2036 |         // 1) Local variables that are assigned in inner scopes ("escaped vars")
2037 |         //
2038 |         // 2) Local variables that are assigned more than once.
2039 |         //
2040 |         // We treat all global variables as escaped by default, so there's
2041 |         // no reason to do this extra computation for them.
2042 |         return;
2043 |       }
2044 | 
2045 |       if (n.isName() && NodeUtil.isLValue(n) &&
2046 |           // Be careful of bleeding functions, which create variables
2047 |           // in the inner scope, not the scope where the name appears.
2048 |           !NodeUtil.isBleedingFunctionName(n)) {
2049 |         String name = n.getString();
2050 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
2051 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
2052 |         if (var != null) {
2053 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
2054 |           if (ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2055 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode()).recordAssignedName(name);
2056 |           }
2057 | 
2058 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2059 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode()).recordEscapedVarName(name);
2060 |           }
2061 |         }
2062 |       } else if (n.isGetProp() && n.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
2063 |           NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
2064 |         String name = NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(n).getString();
2065 |         Scope scope = t.getScope();
2066 |         Var var = scope.getVar(name);
2067 |         if (var != null) {
2068 |           Scope ownerScope = var.getScope();
2069 |           if (scope != ownerScope && ownerScope.isLocal()) {
2070 |             data.get(ownerScope.getRootNode())
2071 |                 .recordEscapedQualifiedName(n.getQualifiedName());
2072 |           }
2073 |         }
2074 |       }
2075 |     }

/**
   * Looks for a parameter type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @param lineno The line of the type expression.
   * @param startCharno The starting character position of the type expression.
   * @param matchingLC Whether the type expression starts with a "{".
   * @param onlyParseSimpleNames If true, only simple type names are parsed
   *     (via a call to parseTypeNameAnnotation instead of
   *     parseTypeExpressionAnnotation).
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1184 |   private Node parseAndRecordTypeNode(JsDocToken token, int lineno,
1185 |                                       int startCharno,
1186 |                                       boolean matchingLC,
1187 |                                       boolean onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1188 |     Node typeNode = null;
1189 | 
1190 |     if (onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1191 |       typeNode = parseTypeNameAnnotation(token);
1192 |     } else {
1193 |       typeNode = parseTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1194 |     }
1195 | 
1196 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1197 |       int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1198 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1199 | 
1200 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(
1201 |           typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endLineno, endCharno, matchingLC);
1202 |     }
1203 | 
1204 |     return typeNode;
1205 |   }

===== M0800 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.JSType(JSTypeRegistry,ImmutableList<String>,ImmutableList<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 114-144
Documentation:

Method code:
114 |   JSType(JSTypeRegistry registry, ImmutableList<String> templateKeys,
115 |       ImmutableList<JSType> templatizedTypes) {
116 |     this.registry = registry;
117 | 
118 |     // Do sanity checking on the specified keys and templatized types.
119 |     int keysLength = templateKeys == null ? 0 : templateKeys.size();
120 |     int typesLength = templatizedTypes == null ? 0 : templatizedTypes.size();
121 |     if (typesLength > keysLength) {
122 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(
123 |           "Cannot have more templatized types than template keys");
124 |     } else if (typesLength < keysLength) {
125 |       // If there are fewer templatized types than keys, extend the templatized
126 |       // types list to match the number of keys, using UNKNOWN_TYPE for the
127 |       // unspecified types.
128 |       ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> builder = ImmutableList.builder();
129 |       if (typesLength > 0) {
130 |         builder.addAll(templatizedTypes);
131 |       }
132 |       for (int i = 0; i < keysLength - typesLength; i++) {
133 |         builder.add(registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE));
134 |       }
135 |       templatizedTypes = builder.build();
136 |     } else if (keysLength == 0 && typesLength == 0) {
137 |       // Ensure that both lists are non-null.
138 |       templateKeys = ImmutableList.of();
139 |       templatizedTypes = ImmutableList.of();
140 |     }
141 | 
142 |     this.templateKeys = templateKeys;
143 |     this.templatizedTypes = templatizedTypes;
144 |   }

===== M0823 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 596-610
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
596 |   public Scope getScope() {
597 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
598 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
599 |       return scope;
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
603 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
604 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
605 |       scopes.push(scope);
606 |     }
607 |     scopeRoots.clear();
608 | 
609 |     return scope;
610 |   }
